<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/3/default-hard-budget-caps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re going to need default hard budget caps on pretty much everything</span><span class='zh'>我们需要对几乎所有事情设定默认的硬性预算上限</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments. The piece argues for automatic limits on compute, storage, and spending, plus tooling to enforce caps without stifling creativity. It positions budget discipline as essential for sustainable experimentation in AI and cloud-native stacks.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。文章提出在计算、存储等方面设定自动约束，并提供执行工具，既控制支出又不完全扼杀创新，强调预算约束在可持续创新中的作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dataforprogress.org/blog/2026/9/29/americans-social-activity-has-steeply-declined-in-the-past-30-years" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Americans&#x27; social activity has steeply declined in the past 30 years</span><span class='zh'>过去三十年美国人的社交活动显著下降</span></a><span class="dom">dataforprogress.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital.</span><span class='zh'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital. The trend raises questions about mental health, community resilience, and how digital life shapes social behavior.</span><span class='zh'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。此趋势引发对心理健康、社区韧性以及数字生活如何塑造社交行为的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/physics/2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobel Prize in Physics 2026: Francis Halzen</span><span class='zh'>2026年诺贝尔物理学奖：弗朗西斯·哈尔岑</span></a><span class="dom">nobelprize.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector. His contributions helped establish high-energy neutrinos as a major new window on the cosmos, enabling discoveries about distant cosmic sources.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。他的工作把高能中微子确立为研究宇宙的新窗口，推动了对遥远宇宙源头的理解。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1097401/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Several vulnerabilities have been discovered in the Linux kernel</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核曝出多项漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage. Patch availability and response timing will vary by distribution, so admins should test updates promptly. The disclosure underscores the ongoing drive to harden core OS components against emerging threat vectors.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。各发行版的修复时间不同，系统管理员应尽快测试并应用更新。此次披露凸显持续对核心操作系统组件进行加固的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/the-dollar63-billion-binance-hack-was-pulled-off-in-just-20-minutes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The $6.3B Binance hack was pulled off in just 20 minutes</span><span class='zh'>价值 63 亿美元的币安黑客攻击仅用时 20 分钟</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hackers siphoned roughly $6.3 billion in cryptocurrency from wallets belonging to Binance across multiple blockchains in about 20 minutes.</span><span class='zh'>黑客在约 20 分钟内从币安所属的钱包中跨多个区块链转走了约 63 亿美元的加密货币。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hackers siphoned roughly $6.3 billion in cryptocurrency from wallets belonging to Binance across multiple blockchains in about 20 minutes. Binance suspended deposits and withdrawals and began investigating, while the exchange and other crypto firms said they had frozen around half of the stolen funds. Much of the remaining cryptocurrency may not be recoverable through freezing.</span><span class='zh'>黑客在约 20 分钟内从币安所属的钱包中跨多个区块链转走了约 63 亿美元的加密货币。币安暂停了存款和提现并展开调查；该交易所及其他加密行业公司称，已冻结约一半被盗资金。剩余的大部分加密货币可能无法通过冻结追回。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic reportedly trained Claude on pirated books from Stanford professors, raising ethics concerns and potential legal risks.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Anthropic 使用了从斯坦福教授处非法获取的书籍来训练 Claude，这引发伦理担忧并可能带来法律风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://claude.com/product/claude-duet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Duet</span><span class='zh'>Claude Duet：对话协作模型</span></a><span class="dom">claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Duet enables two Claude agents to collaborate, with one handling planning and the other execution, improving reliability and handling complex tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Duet 让两个 Claude 代理协同工作，一个负责计划、一个执行，提升任务可靠性并分担复杂推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Duet enables two Claude agents to collaborate, with one handling planning and the other execution, improving reliability and handling complex tasks. This duet approach aims to reduce hallucinations and offload hard reasoning, making AI copilots more practical for real work.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Duet 让两个 Claude 代理协同工作，一个负责计划、一个执行，提升任务可靠性并分担复杂推理。通过二重协作的方式，这种设计旨在减少幻觉现象并提升对复杂任务的处理能力，使 AI 助手在实际场景中更具实用性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theranos.world/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Theranos.world</span><span class='zh'>Theranos.world</span></a><span class="dom">theranos.world</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation. The piece underscores the ongoing challenge creators face in maintaining control over distributed software.</span><span class='zh'>开发者指出尽管提出下架请求，GitHub 仍未清除其软件的破解副本，暴露了执法与平台治理之间的矛盾及版权保护的难题。文章强调创作者在分发软件时维权的持续挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102102/0c00e4d550f25f8c/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Python&#x27;s experimental JIT to be removed</span><span class='zh'>Python 实验性 JIT 将被移除</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Python core developers plan to remove the experimental JIT after evaluating its benefits and maintenance burden.</span><span class='zh'>CPython 的实验性 JIT 将被移除，原因在于实际收益不足以覆盖维护成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Python core developers plan to remove the experimental JIT after evaluating its benefits and maintenance burden. The decision suggests Python will focus on language-level optimizations and alternative runtimes, while users needing JIT performance should rely on projects like PyPy or other tooling.</span><span class='zh'>CPython 的实验性 JIT 将被移除，原因在于实际收益不足以覆盖维护成本。这意味着 Python 将继续以解释器层优化为主，用户若需要 JIT 性能可转向 PyPy 等替代实现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/chatgpt-is-adding-real-cartoonists-signatures-to-fake-new-yorker-cartoons/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ChatGPT is adding real cartoonists&#x27; signatures to fake New Yorker cartoons</span><span class='zh'>ChatGPT 将真实漫画家的签名添加到假冒的《纽约客》漫画中</span></a><span class="dom">niemanlab.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur. This development underscores the challenges of provenance in AI-generated art and the potential need for watermarking or policy safeguards.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reflection AI rolls out Beam 501B as an open-weight model, allowing researchers to run a large language model locally without gateways.</span><span class='zh'>Reflection AI 发布 Beam 501B 开放权重模型，允许研究者在本地运行大型语言模型，降低对远端服务的依赖。</span></p>

This expands access and invites community evaluation, with emphasis on safety and alignment considerations.</span><span class='zh'>Reflection AI 发布 Beam 501B 开放权重模型，允许研究者在本地运行大型语言模型，降低对远端服务的依赖。开放权重将推动研究者协同评估，并强调安全与对齐的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.vanityfair.com/story/jared-kushner-hacked-phone-secret-negotiations" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jared Kushner Hacked Phone Reveals Secret Negotiations, Millions in Transactions</span><span class='zh'>贾里德·库什纳被入侵的手机曝光秘密谈判与数百万交易</span></a><span class="dom">vanityfair.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hacked phone belonging to a high-profile figure reveals alleged secret negotiations and large-scale financial transfers, underscoring how personal devices can leak sensitive diplomacy.</span><span class='zh'>据称属于知名人物的被入侵手机曝光了秘密谈判和大额交易记录，凸显个人设备可能泄露敏感外交信息的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hacked phone belonging to a high-profile figure reveals alleged secret negotiations and large-scale financial transfers, underscoring how personal devices can leak sensitive diplomacy. The breach highlights security risks for top officials and the potential consequences of private data leaking into public view. It also raises questions about how such disclosures could reshape power dynamics and influence campaigns abroad.</span><span class='zh'>据称属于知名人物的被入侵手机曝光了秘密谈判和大额交易记录，凸显个人设备可能泄露敏感外交信息的风险。此事暴露出高层安全风险以及私人数据若外泄可能带来的后果。也引发对此类披露如何影响权力格局和国际博弈的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.kadoa.com/energy-prices/insights/investor-owned-utilities" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Investor-owned utilities charge 23% more for electricity than public providers</span><span class='zh'>私营公用事业向用户收取的电价比公立提供者高出23%</span></a><span class="dom">kadoa.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Electricity prices charged by investor-owned utilities run about 23% higher than public providers.</span><span class='zh'>投资者所有的公用事业公司电价比公营提供者高出约23%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Electricity prices charged by investor-owned utilities run about 23% higher than public providers. The gap highlights how privatization and regulatory structures influence consumer costs, even as reliability and service quality may vary. For tech and energy teams budgeting for grid usage and energy-intensive workloads, the differential matters for planning and resilience.</span><span class='zh'>投资者所有的公用事业公司电价比公营提供者高出约23%。这一差距揭示了私有化与监管框架如何影响消费者成本，即便可靠性和服务质量也会有所不同。对于需要用电的技术团队和能源密集型工作负载而言，这一差异在预算、规划与韧性设计上具有显著影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes a city-building game that foregrounds bureaucratic friction and civic pushback, where the city environment actively complicates development. Players must navigate red tape, budget constraints, and unpopular policies, turning urban planning into a tense strategy challenge.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一款强调官僚阻力与市政反对的城市建造游戏，城市环境会主动让开发变得复杂。玩家需应对繁文缭节的行政流程、预算约束和不受欢迎的政策，使城市规划成为紧张的策略对决。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-03266-x" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bleached corals thrive within days when touching healthy neighbour</span><span class='zh'>接触健康邻居后，白化珊瑚数日内快速恢复</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals. The interaction likely involves beneficial chemical signals or metabolite exchange that jump-starts recovery and resilience. The finding highlights potential reef restoration strategies that leverage proximity or graft-like approaches to accelerate recovery.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。这一现象可能来自健康珊瑚释放的有益信号分子或代谢物的转移，帮助受损珊瑚重新获得活性。此发现为珊瑚礁修复提供了新的思路，如通过近邻效应或嫁接等方法加速康复。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20261019-birdwatching-rewires-the-brain-and-slows-cognitive-decline" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Birdwatching rewires the brain and slows cognitive decline</span><span class='zh'>观鸟能重新塑造大脑并减缓认知衰退</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Watching birds engages attention and memory networks, promoting neuroplasticity in aging brains.</span><span class='zh'>观鸟能激活大脑的注意力与记忆网络，促进老年人群的神经可塑性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Watching birds engages attention and memory networks, promoting neuroplasticity in aging brains. Emerging evidence suggests nature-based, cognitively engaging hobbies may help slow cognitive decline and support mental well-being.</span><span class='zh'>观鸟能激活大脑的注意力与记忆网络，促进老年人群的神经可塑性。日益增多的证据表明，参与自然和认知挑战性的爱好可能有助于减缓认知衰退并提升心理健康。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/oosexgang/hackios-xnu-kernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Run full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android Devices</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在 Android 设备上运行完整的 iOS 16.5 固件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on project demonstrates booting and running the full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android hardware, challenging cross-OS compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>一个动手项目展示在 Android 硬件上跑完整的 iOS 16.5 固件，挑战跨操作系统兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on project demonstrates booting and running the full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android hardware, challenging cross-OS compatibility. It highlights the iOS boot chain, kernel and driver constraints, and what the exercise reveals about the barriers between mobile ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>一个动手项目展示在 Android 硬件上跑完整的 iOS 16.5 固件，挑战跨操作系统兼容性。该工作揭示了 iOS 启动链、内核与驱动的约束，以及两大移动生态之间的壁垒。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nordwand Mono is a new monospaced font designed for coders and adventurers, aiming for high legibility and distinctive personality in IDEs and terminals. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TechDirt&#x27;s piece argues that relying on a single gatekeeper for reach—like Facebook&#x27;s megaphone—is risky. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://block.xyz/dessa" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dessa: Conversation Model trained only on speech</span><span class='zh'>Dessa：仅用语音训练的对话模型</span></a><span class="dom">block.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An conversation model trained exclusively on spoken language data tests how far dialog capabilities can emerge without text-based training.</span><span class='zh'>仅使用语音数据训练的对话模型用于测试在没有文本训练的情况下，对话能力能够达到何种水平。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An conversation model trained exclusively on spoken language data tests how far dialog capabilities can emerge without text-based training. The work highlights strengths in speech understanding and prosody handling, while revealing gaps in reasoning and abstract knowledge.</span><span class='zh'>仅使用语音数据训练的对话模型用于测试在没有文本训练的情况下，对话能力能够达到何种水平。结果显示在语音理解和语调处理方面具备优势，同时在推理和抽象知识方面存在不足。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://carmelitegothic.com/cnc-stone-carving/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CNC Stone Carving</span><span class='zh'>CNC 石材雕刻</span></a><span class="dom">carmelitegothic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CNC stone carving blends automation with traditional craftsmanship, enabling precise, repeatable stonework for architecture and sculpture.</span><span class='zh'>CNC 石材雕刻将自动化与传统工艺结合，实现建筑与雕塑领域的高精度、可重复加工。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CNC stone carving blends automation with traditional craftsmanship, enabling precise, repeatable stonework for architecture and sculpture.</span><span class='zh'>CNC 石材雕刻将自动化与传统工艺结合，实现建筑与雕塑领域的高精度、可重复加工。内容涵盖工作流、工具选型以及材料要点，以提升雕刻质量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/OpenShell" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenShell – A safe, private runtime for fleets of autonomous AI agents</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell：为成群自主 AI 代理提供安全、私有的运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenShell provides a private, sandboxed runtime to run fleets of autonomous AI agents with strong isolation and governance.</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell 提供私有、沙箱化的运行时环境，用于部署成群的自主 AI 代理，强调强隔离与治理。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Late AOSP source releases are spurring developers to reverse-engineer libraries to report bugs, underscoring the tension between open-source transparency and practical debugging.</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布延迟，迫使开发者通过反汇编库来定位并报告漏洞，凸显开源透明度与实际调试之间的矛盾与挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron：开放源代码的微型3D打印平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision. The project offers mechanical designs, electronics, and firmware to enable precision micro-manufacturing at a hobbyist level. It showcases how open hardware can democratize advanced fabrication capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。该项目提供机械设计、电子和固件，面向爱好者层面的微型制造。它展示了开源硬件如何让高端加工能力走向大众。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microg/GmsCore/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MicroG: Free-libre re-implementation of Google&#x27;s proprietary Android user space</span><span class='zh'>MicroG：对 Google Android 用户空间的自由开源实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries. It supports many apps and services while prioritizing privacy, empowering custom ROMs and privacy-focused devices, though some apps may rely on proprietary features.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。尽管兼容性可能有限，但它为隐私友好的设备与自定义 ROM 提供了可行选项，某些应用仍可能依赖专有特性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/franzenzenhofer/big-arrow-on-the-screen" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Let your AI agents paint big arrows, boxes and text on your screen</span><span class='zh'>让 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A lightweight demo shows AI agents annotating the UI with large arrows, boxes, and labels to explain their plans in real time.</span><span class='zh'>一个轻量级演示展示 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本标签，以实时解释其计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A lightweight demo shows AI agents annotating the UI with large arrows, boxes, and labels to explain their plans in real time. Such overlays boost interpretability and debugging, helping humans understand where the agent is headed and why. The approach is easy to prototype and could be used in demos, tutorials, or human-in-the-loop workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个轻量级演示展示 AI 代理在屏幕上绘制大箭头、方框和文本标签，以实时解释其计划。此类覆盖能提升可解释性与调试效果，帮助人类理解代理的意图与下一步动作。该方法易于原型化，适用于演示、教程和人机协作场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/szabadkai/c64-keyboard-font/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A font recreated from photographs of classic Commodore 64 keycaps</span><span class='zh'>从经典 Commodore 64 键帽照片重现的字体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design.</span><span class='zh'>一个字体完全由经典 Commodore 64 键帽的照片重建而成，展示了通过摄影测量和图像方法推动字体设计的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design. It offers a retro aesthetic for UI or art and shows creative ways to derive glyph shapes from real-world objects.</span><span class='zh'>一个字体完全由经典 Commodore 64 键帽的照片重建而成，展示了通过摄影测量和图像方法推动字体设计的可能性。为 UI/艺术提供复古美感，并展示了从真实物件派生字形的创意路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>QGIS enacts moratorium on AI contributions</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 对 AI 贡献实施暂停</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed. The move signals open-source communities grappling with risk and quality control as AI tooling becomes more integrated. It may slow AI-assisted features in the GIS project but could set precedent for other OSS projects.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。此举显示开源社区在 AI 工具日益整合的环境下面临的风险与质量控制挑战，可能放慢该 GIS 项目中 AI 助力的功能部署，但也可能为其他开源项目树立先例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-pytorch/torch_tpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch back end for TPUs</span><span class='zh'>Google 开源 TorchTPU：面向 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users. This lowers the barrier to running PyTorch models on Google&#x27;s accelerators and could shift performance comparisons across ML stacks.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。此举降低了在 TPU 上运行 PyTorch 的门槛，可能改变不同 ML 堆栈间的性能对比。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cobanov/PS5LM" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Llama.cpp on a jailbroken PS5: run any GGUF model natively on the console</span><span class='zh'>在越狱 PS5 上运行 Llama.cpp：在主机本地运行任何 GGUF 模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware.</span><span class='zh'>社区移植了 llama.cpp 到越狱的 PS5，允许在主机硬件上本地运行 GGUF 格式的模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware. This demonstrates a practical path for local, low-cost model deployment on high-performance consumer hardware—at the cost of requiring a jailbroken device and dealing with memory/thermal limits.</span><span class='zh'>社区移植了 llama.cpp 到越狱的 PS5，允许在主机硬件上本地运行 GGUF 格式的模型。该项目展示了用高性能消费级设备实现本地低成本推理的可行性，但需要越狱设备并需应对内存与散热等限制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Florilegium: Procedural Flowers in LaTeX</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium：用 LaTeX 生成程序化花卉装饰</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua. It combines hand-authored primitives, randomized parameters, and a procedural layout that fills unused space. The creator says the project produces a similar look to a referenced design without trying to replicate it, and used Claude during development.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。它结合手工编写的基本图形、随机参数和用于填充空白区域的程序化布局。作者表示，该项目形成了与某个参考设计相似的效果，但并未试图复制原作，开发过程中使用了 Claude。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>366</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>53</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-49985664" data-id="49985664" data-tags="Programming|Legal|Hardware|Code" data-score="363" data-comments="307" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49985664" data-time="1791329288">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/boykopovar/AnyPS5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AnyPS5: Port PS5 binaries to PC without emulation (87% system libraries mapped)</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5：在不使用模拟的情况下将 PS5 二进制移植到 PC（87% 系统库已映射）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation. The project showcases progress in cross-architecture porting but raises legal, licensing, and anti-tampering questions as it matures.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。该项目展示了跨架构移植的进展，但随着成熟，也引发法律、授权和反篡改等方面的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kksimp/Mac.apk" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android apps running native on macOS</span><span class='zh'>Android 应用在 macOS 上原生运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates running Android apps natively on macOS, potentially removing emulation overhead.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示在 macOS 上原生运行 Android 应用，或许能消除仿真开销。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates running Android apps natively on macOS, potentially removing emulation overhead. If mature, this could blur platform boundaries for developers and users, enabling smoother access to Android ecosystems on Apple Silicon.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示在 macOS 上原生运行 Android 应用，或许能消除仿真开销。若成熟，将模糊开发者与用户的跨平台边界，使 Android 生态在 Apple Silicon 上获得更顺畅的体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/history.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI Withdraws 3 Math Papers</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 撤回三篇数学论文</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI has withdrawn three math papers from its official archive, citing the need for corrections or updates.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已从其数学论文存档撤下三篇论文，原因在于需要纠正或更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI has withdrawn three math papers from its official archive, citing the need for corrections or updates. The withdrawals highlight ongoing scrutiny of rigor and reproducibility in AI-aligned mathematical research, and point to the importance of versioning and clear disclosures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 已从其数学论文存档撤下三篇论文，原因在于需要纠正或更新。此举凸显了围绕 AI 相关数学研究的严格性与可复现性持续受关注，强调版本控制和明确披露的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Zagitta/gpu-unlock" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPU-unlock: FP/BF16 instruction issue rate on Nvidia 3000&#x27;s cards</span><span class='zh'>GPU-unlock：NVIDIA 30系列显卡的 FP/BF16 指令发射率问题</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community project analyzes FP and BF16 instruction issue rates on Nvidia RTX 30-series cards, suggesting bottlenecks that affect mixed-precision workloads.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区项目对 NVIDIA RTX 30 系列显卡的 FP 与 BF16 指令发射率进行分析，揭示对混合精度任务的潜在瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community project analyzes FP and BF16 instruction issue rates on Nvidia RTX 30-series cards, suggesting bottlenecks that affect mixed-precision workloads. The findings could inform driver optimizations, compiler choices, and how developers allocate precision in AI and HPC tasks.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区项目对 NVIDIA RTX 30 系列显卡的 FP 与 BF16 指令发射率进行分析，揭示对混合精度任务的潜在瓶颈。相关结果可能为驱动优化、编译器选择以及开发者在 AI 和 HPC 任务中对精度的分配提供参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FeSens/openTPU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenTPU – An open-source AI accelerator, developed by AI</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU：一个开源的AI加速器项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures. By inviting community contributions, it aims to democratize access to AI hardware and accelerate experimentation beyond vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。通过鼓励社区贡献，它旨在降低对厂商锁定的依赖，推动 AI 硬件的普及与创新。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rociiu/talorys" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Talorys – A self-hosted personal AI agent on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier</span><span class='zh'>Talorys：在 Cloudflare 免费层自托管的个人 AI 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription. It demonstrates how individuals can deploy private AI tools at the edge, balancing privacy, latency, and cost while avoiding vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。它展示了个人在边缘部署私有 AI 工具的可能性，平衡隐私、低延迟和成本，同时降低对单一厂商的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chris124567/cfsolve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile solver library (no headless browser required)</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 求解库（无需无头浏览器）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser. It returns a Turnstile token for widgets or a cf_clearance cookie for challenge pages. Widgets can be handled with a page URL and site key, while challenge pages require a direct fetch or the page’s HTML.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。它会为小部件返回 Turnstile 令牌，为挑战页面返回 cf_clearance Cookie。处理小部件需要页面 URL 和站点密钥，而挑战页面则需要直接抓取页面或提供页面 HTML。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>292</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277379' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>96</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50306442" data-id="50306442" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Programming|Hardware|Gaming|Code" data-score="280" data-comments="141" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50306442" data-time="1793378422">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/dbreunig/nesformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">501</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dbreunig/nesformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A working Nintendo in ~1GB of Qwen weights</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在约 1GB 的 Qwen 权重中运行一台可工作的任天堂主机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model. The NES 6502 CPU, 2 KB of work RAM, and cartridge ROM run inside the model checkpoint, with every weight computed by a compiler rather than learned through training. During play, the model predicts the CPU execution log token by token while a host program handles graphics, controls, and the vertical-blank interrupt.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。NES 的 6502 CPU、2 KB 工作内存和卡带程序 ROM 都运行在这个模型检查点内，所有权重均由编译器计算得出，而不是通过训练学习。游戏运行时，模型逐 token 预测 CPU 执行日志，主机程序则负责绘图、读取手柄输入和触发垂直消隐中断。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>280</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50306442' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50228115" data-id="50228115" data-tags="Show HN|Hardware|Open Source|Code" data-score="277" data-comments="99" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50228115" data-time="1792900076">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/notaroomba/keybytes" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard showcases a build with swappable parts and open hardware ethos.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘展示了一个具备可替换部件、开放软硬件理念的设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard showcases a build with swappable parts and open hardware ethos. It demonstrates how design-for-modularity can extend device lifespans and tailor ergonomics. The project invites readers to rethink how keyboards can evolve with users.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘展示了一个具备可替换部件、开放软硬件理念的设计。此类模块化设计能延长设备寿命并更好地满足用户的打字需求。该项目促使人们重新思考键盘如何随用户演进。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In the SlopCodeBench 10/2026 report, Opus 5.5 leads the coding benchmarks while Astra posts the strongest overall score. The assessment also marks xhigh as harmful, highlighting ongoing safety and reliability debates for coding agents and the value of diverse benchmarking</span><span class='zh'>在 SlopCodeBench 2026年10月的评测中，Opus 5.5领跑代码基准测试，Astra在总分上表现最优。同时将 xhigh 标记为有害，凸显编码代理在安全性与可靠性方面的持续讨论以及多维基准的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/pylance-release/blob/main/docs/tsp/type-server-protocol.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type Server Protocol</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，扩展语言服务器协议</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol. The protocol enables editors and language servers to exchange richer typing information and state, improving performance and scalability for multi-language toolchains. This move could help unify editor experiences across languages and tooling ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。此举有望提升多语言工具链的性能与可扩展性，并统一编辑器体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maanHimself/OpenDLSS-NR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS: A Vulkan Reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 Neural Rendering Network</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR：基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS-NR is a Vulkan-based open-source reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 neural rendering network, aiming to bring similar upscaling quality to a wider set of GPUs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR 是基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络的开源实现，目标是在更广泛的硬件上提供类似的超分辨能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS-NR is a Vulkan-based open-source reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 neural rendering network, aiming to bring similar upscaling quality to a wider set of GPUs. If successful, it could empower developers to integrate neural upscaling into Vulkan apps without relying on Nvidia&#x27;s stack, but performance and fidelity benchmarks remain uncertain.</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR 是基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络的开源实现，目标是在更广泛的硬件上提供类似的超分辨能力。若成熟可让开发者在 Vulkan 应用中实现神经上采样，而不必依赖 Nvidia 的专有栈，但性能和画质的基准仍在发展中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Saffsanity/sill" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad</span><span class='zh'>让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices. The project showcases a path toward seamless multi-device workflows, though early-stage and experimental. It hints at a future where a single desktop session spans the Apple ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。该项目凸显了未来可能的无缝多设备工作流，但在实际应用前还需克服稳定性与性能挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/home-assistant/home-assistant.io/issues/49314" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I never authorized an integration that scans my secrets</span><span class='zh'>我从未授权的集成在扫描我的秘密</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent. The incident highlights privacy risks in extensible platforms and the need for explicit consent and auditable secrets handling. It underscores the ongoing tension between platform openness and user data protection in open-source ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。此事暴露了可扩展平台中的隐私风险，也强调需要对 secrets 的处理进行显式同意和可审计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI CLI</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 命令行界面</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI released an official command-line interface, letting developers interact with models, manage API keys, and automate tasks from the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布官方命令行界面，方便开发者在终端与模型交互、管理 API Key 以及执行自动化任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI released an official command-line interface, letting developers interact with models, manage API keys, and automate tasks from the terminal. The CLI lowers barriers for scripting, automation, and CI workflows, complementing the Python client.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布官方命令行界面，方便开发者在终端与模型交互、管理 API Key 以及执行自动化任务。CLI 降低了上手门槛，适合在脚本、持续集成等场景使用，与 Python 客户端形成互补。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/da-luce/astroterm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astroterm: A Planetarium for Your Terminal</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm：让你的终端成为行星仪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell. It brings rich visualization to environments without a full GUI, making it a handy tool for quick stargazing and education.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。即使在无完整图形界面的环境下，也能进行直观的天文可视化，适合快速观星与学习。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SheepSeb/tinyworldformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A tiny world model running games on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上运行的微型世界模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers. The demo demonstrates memory-efficient planning and small neural models that fit in ultra‑compact hardware, hinting at future autonomous nodes in IoT. It signals potential for smarter, low-power devices at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。该演示表明在资源受限设备上也能运行简短的神经模型和规划任务。预示着物联网边缘设备将拥有更智能的能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maxilevi/project-hedra/tree/pre-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game I wrote from scratch while in highschool</span><span class='zh'>Hedra：高中时从零写成的 3D 开放世界游戏</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game created from scratch in high school.</span><span class='zh'>Hedra 是作者在高中时独自从零开始开发的 3D 开放世界游戏。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game created from scratch in high school. This solo project demonstrates independent game development skills, including world-building, controls, and basic engine work, offering a peek into a teen&#x27;s indie effort.</span><span class='zh'>Hedra 是作者在高中时独自从零开始开发的 3D 开放世界游戏。该作品展示了单人开发的完整流程，包括世界构建、控制与基础引擎工作，呈现青少年独立游戏开发的有趣案例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/KrushnaVardhanReddy/PaperPilot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PaperPilot – Zero-Cloud, Pure-Rust PDF Engine with WASM and MCP</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot：零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持 WASM 与 MCP</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PaperPilot is a zero-cloud, pure-Rust PDF engine designed to run in WebAssembly with MCP support.</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot 是一个零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持在 WebAssembly 中运行并配合 MCP 使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PaperPilot is a zero-cloud, pure-Rust PDF engine designed to run in WebAssembly with MCP support. It enables offline, self-contained PDF processing that can be embedded into apps without external services. This could be appealing for privacy-focused workflows and edge deployments.</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot 是一个零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持在 WebAssembly 中运行并配合 MCP 使用。它实现离线、即可嵌入的 PDF 处理，避免对外部服务的依赖。对注重隐私的工作流和边缘部署尤具吸引力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ducklake is a DuckDB project introducing data-lake integration that enables querying across data lakes with DuckDB.</span><span class='zh'>Ducklake 是 DuckDB 的数据湖集成项目，使用户能够用 DuckDB 跨数据湖进行查询分析。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ducklake is a DuckDB project introducing data-lake integration that enables querying across data lakes with DuckDB. It demonstrates how DuckDB can bridge lakehouse data sources for analytics.</span><span class='zh'>Ducklake 是 DuckDB 的数据湖集成项目，使用户能够用 DuckDB 跨数据湖进行查询分析。它展示了 DuckDB 在连接湖仓数据源以实现分析的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antirez/c64-hw-simulator" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Commodore 64 hardware simulator: break chips and pins, see the results</span><span class='zh'>Commodore 64 硬件模拟器：解析芯片与引脚，观察结果</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate. The project illustrates hardware-level behavior and can aid understanding of retro systems, fault tolerance, and emulation accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。该项目有助于理解硬件层面的行为、复古系统的仿真精度，以及对容错设计的研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shaongitbd/doory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Doory – Open-Source Canva</span><span class='zh'>Doory：开源的 Canva 替代工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options.</span><span class='zh'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options. It represents a privacy-friendly, self-hostable alternative for teams and individuals who want control over their design pipeline.</span><span class='zh'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。它为注重隐私、希望自托管设计流程的团队与个人提供了一个可控的替代选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/valimised/ivxv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Source code of the Estonian online voting system</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统源代码公开</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research.</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research. This openness can improve transparency and help identify vulnerabilities, though online voting remains a debated topic.</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。这一举措有望提升透明度并帮助发现潜在漏洞，但在线投票的安全性与隐私性仍是讨论焦点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/prabanta-dev/janas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Janas: Run LLMs larger than RAM by streaming experts from SSD</span><span class='zh'>Janas：通过从 SSD 流式加载专家参数，在 RAM 之外运行超大规模 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed. By using a mixture-of-experts design, it fetches parts of the model only when required, reducing memory pressure without needing massive hardware upgrades. The approach could lower the bar for experimenting with large models on commodity machines.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。它利用专家模型的分布式架构，只有在需要时才获取相应参数，降低对内存的压力，无需巨额硬件升级。该思路若规模化，将降低在普通硬件上尝试大模型的门槛。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A PSP port of God of War has been recompiled to WebAssembly and runs in the browser, highlighting the cross-platform portability of wasm and the potential for retro games in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>一款在 PSP 上的《God of War》被重新编译为 WebAssembly，并在浏览器中运行，展示了 wasm 的跨平台可移植性与复古游戏在浏览器中的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A PSP port of God of War has been recompiled to WebAssembly and runs in the browser, highlighting the cross-platform portability of wasm and the potential for retro games in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>一款在 PSP 上的《God of War》被重新编译为 WebAssembly，并在浏览器中运行，展示了 wasm 的跨平台可移植性与复古游戏在浏览器中的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator – A sub-micrometer 3D motion control plattform</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator：亚微米级三维运动控制平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware. The project aims to democratize precision manipulation for research and education, enabling ultra-fine control in labs and makerspaces.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful experimental debugger that applies Nietzschean prompts to guide debugging strategies, offering a philosophical UX to debugging workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful experimental debugger that applies Nietzschean prompts to guide debugging strategies, offering a philosophical UX to debugging workflows. It’s a thought-provoking take on making debugging less mechanical and more narrative.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。它挑战传统的机械式排错，试图让调试过程更具叙事性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ihhub/fheroes2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>fheroes2: A recreation of Heroes of Might and Magic II game engine</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2：对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》游戏引擎的重建</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>fheroes2 is an open-source recreation of the Heroes of Might and Magic II engine, aiming to faithfully reproduce classic gameplay across platforms.</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2 是对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》引擎的开源重建，目标在各平台上忠实再现经典玩法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>fheroes2 is an open-source recreation of the Heroes of Might and Magic II engine, aiming to faithfully reproduce classic gameplay across platforms. The project modernizes the engine while preserving the original&#x27;s look and feel, enabling new audiences to experience the game. It demonstrates how community-driven reverse-engineering can extend retro games.</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2 是对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》引擎的开源重建，目标在各平台上忠实再现经典玩法。项目在保留原作风格的同时实现引擎现代化，帮助新玩家体验这款经典游戏。它展示了社区驱动的逆向工程如何延续复古游戏的生命力。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">720</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/timescale/pg_vectorsearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch: An index that is faster than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch：比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快的索引</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database. Embedding an optimized index into Postgres can simplify architecture by avoiding external vector stores while improving search speed for vector-heavy workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。把高效索引嵌入 Postgres 可以减少对外部向量存储的依赖，同时为向量密集型负载提升检索速度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/14/top_stories_10142026'><span class='en'>Oct 14</span><span class='zh'>10月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>206</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086165' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>52</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-49911995" data-id="49911995" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Startups|Code" data-score="194" data-comments="97" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49911995" data-time="1790789860">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/magnitudedev/magnitude" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – Self-optimizing inference engine for agents</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – 面向代理的自我优化推理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Magnitude is a self-optimizing inference engine designed for agents, automatically tuning runtimes and workloads to boost performance and efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>Magnitude 是一个面向代理的自我优化推理引擎，能够自动调参以提升推理性能和能效，减少人工调优工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Magnitude is a self-optimizing inference engine designed for agents, automatically tuning runtimes and workloads to boost performance and efficiency. It sits at the intersection of AI agent frameworks and efficient inference, promising to reduce manual tuning and hardware waste. Backed by YC S25, it signals growing interest in tooling for autonomous agents.</span><span class='zh'>Magnitude 是一个面向代理的自我优化推理引擎，能够自动调参以提升推理性能和能效，减少人工调优工作。它处在 AI 代理框架与高效推理技术的交汇处，代表了为自走代理提升算力利用率的一条新路径。由 YC S25 支持，反映了对自动化代理工具的热切关注。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/09/30/top_stories_09302026'><span class='en'>Sep 30</span><span class='zh'>9月30日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>194</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49911995' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>97</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50195488" data-id="50195488" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Code" data-score="194" data-comments="83" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195488" data-time="1792677884">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dsmurrell/onkey" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OnKey – I&#x27;m a bad singer, so I built an app to show where I&#x27;m off-key</span><span class='zh'>OnKey：我走音，于是做了个应用标出走音点</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OnKey analyzes your singing pitch in real time and marks off-key moments, turning practice into actionable feedback.</span><span class='zh'>OnKey 实时分析你的歌声音高并标出走音片段，把练习变成可执行的反馈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OnKey analyzes your singing pitch in real time and marks off-key moments, turning practice into actionable feedback. Built as a Show HN project, it demonstrates how accessible audio analysis tools can be built with open-source tech and signal processing. It could inspire more consumer-friendly music-improvement apps.</span><span class='zh'>OnKey 实时分析你的歌声音高并标出走音片段，把练习变成可执行的反馈。作为 Show HN 项目，展示了用开源技术和信号处理构建音乐分析工具的可行性。它或许会激发更多面向大众的发声训练应用的开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/mxc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MXC - a sandboxed code execution system</span><span class='zh'>MXC：一个沙箱化的代码执行系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MXC provides a sandboxed, policy-controlled code execution environment designed to run arbitrary code safely in the cloud.</span><span class='zh'>MXC 提供一个沙箱化、受策略控制的代码执行环境，旨在安全地在云端运行不受信任的代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MXC provides a sandboxed, policy-controlled code execution environment designed to run arbitrary code safely in the cloud. It supports isolating processes, controlling resources, and auditing behavior, enabling safer experimentation and evaluation of untrusted code.</span><span class='zh'>MXC 提供一个沙箱化、受策略控制的代码执行环境，旨在安全地在云端运行不受信任的代码。它通过进程隔离、资源限制和行为审计等机制，便于在云端进行安全的代码评估与试验。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/09/top_stories_10092026'><span class='en'>Oct 9</span><span class='zh'>10月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>192</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50016489' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>83</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50205816" data-id="50205816" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="190" data-comments="148" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205816" data-time="1792742733">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Remove pgrust: the entry is cheating (per-query kernels and result caching)</span><span class='zh'>移除 pgrust：条目被认定为作弊（逐查询内核与结果缓存）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project proposes removing pgrust after indications of cheating, including per-query kernels and result caching that skew benchmarks.</span><span class='zh'>在发现存在作弊迹象（如逐查询内核与结果缓存扭曲基准结果）后，项目组提议移除 pgrust。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project proposes removing pgrust after indications of cheating, including per-query kernels and result caching that skew benchmarks. The move reinforces the need for honest, transparent benchmarking in open-source projects and could affect credibility of reported performance figures. Maintainers emphasize fair competition and reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>在发现存在作弊迹象（如逐查询内核与结果缓存扭曲基准结果）后，项目组提议移除 pgrust。此举强调在开源基准测试中保持公正、透明的重要性，可能影响对性能数据的信任度，并敦促维护者关注可复现性与公平竞争。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/M-Abozaid/esp32-c3-adblock" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ESP32-C3 Adblock</span><span class='zh'>ESP32-C3 广告拦截器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open-source project implements ad-blocking on ESP32-C3 devices, enabling users to run a lightweight network-wide blocker at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个面向 ESP32-C3 的开源广告拦截实现，能够在边缘设备上实现网络级拦截。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open-source project implements ad-blocking on ESP32-C3 devices, enabling users to run a lightweight network-wide blocker at the edge. The approach demonstrates how consumer IoT devices can contribute to privacy and performance by filtering ads and trackers before they reach devices.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个面向 ESP32-C3 的开源广告拦截实现，能够在边缘设备上实现网络级拦截。该方案展示了消费级物联网设备如何通过过滤广告与追踪来提升隐私与性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mikroverk/esp32sim" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Esp32sim – Run ESP32 firmware in the browser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Esp32sim—在浏览器中运行 ESP32 固件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware. It leverages browser-based emulation to emulate the ESP32 environment, reducing setup friction for developers and students. A practical tool for hands-on learning and quick debugging.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。它通过浏览器端的仿真实现 ESP32 环境的模拟，减少开发者和学生的搭建成本。是一个面向动手学习与快速调试的实用工具。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 is released with security fixes and new features, signaling ongoing evolution of the crypto stack.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布，包含安全修复与新特性，体现密码学库的持续演进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 is released with security fixes and new features, signaling ongoing evolution of the crypto stack. It’s a reminder to keep cryptographic dependencies up to date in production systems.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布，包含安全修复与新特性，体现密码学库的持续演进。此次更新可能对某些 API 进行弃用并要求开发者进行兼容性测试，提醒在生产环境中保持加密依赖的最新状态。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/colinhacks/zod/commit/d821685ea6732436fcdf786a81d076958471e467" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zod 4.6.6 compromised by malware?</span><span class='zh'>Zod 4.6.6 是否被恶意软件入侵？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A suspected malware compromise affected a Zod release, raising concerns about supply-chain security for popular open-source libraries.</span><span class='zh'>对流行开源库 Zod 的发行版本怀疑被嵌入恶意软件，引发对软件供应链安全的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A suspected malware compromise affected a Zod release, raising concerns about supply-chain security for popular open-source libraries. The incident underscores the need for reproducible builds, vendor transparency, and rapid incident response in the ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>对流行开源库 Zod 的发行版本怀疑被嵌入恶意软件，引发对软件供应链安全的担忧。事件凸显了可复现构建、供应商透明度和快速事件响应在生态系统中的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/25921" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop continuously generates Crashpad pending dumps</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns. Maintainers are tracking the issue and discussing fixes and mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。维护者正在跟进并讨论修复与缓解措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/picocss/pico/discussions/754" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pico CSS is no longer maintained</span><span class='zh'>Pico CSS 不再维护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility. The post may offer guidance on how to migrate or handle breaking changes while preserving the lightweight, accessible styling Pico CSS is known for.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。文中给出迁移建议与应对潜在改动的途径，同时保留 Pico CSS 的简洁与可访问性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A speculative piece arguing about why conditional constructs like if-then-else are essential in programming language design, exploring historical aspects and potential alternatives. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andreasfertig/cppinsights" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>C++ Insights – See your source code with the eyes of a Compiler</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights：用编译器的视角看你的源代码</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>C++ Insights exposes how your code looks to the compiler, revealing template instantiation and overload resolution in action.</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights 将你的代码呈现为编译器的视角，揭示模板实例化与重载解析等后台过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>C++ Insights exposes how your code looks to the compiler, revealing template instantiation and overload resolution in action. This helps developers understand surprising behavior and write more robust, well-abstracted code.</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights 将你的代码呈现为编译器的视角，揭示模板实例化与重载解析等后台过程。它帮助开发者理解意料之外的行为，并编写更健壮、抽象层次更清晰的代码。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A macOS project demonstrates on-device AI indexing for every photo and video frame, enabling fast, semantic search without cloud data.</span><span class='zh'>一个 macOS 项目展示了在本地对每张照片、每一帧视频进行 AI 索引，以实现快速的语义搜索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A macOS project demonstrates on-device AI indexing for every photo and video frame, enabling fast, semantic search without cloud data. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aaron Ardiri has released PalmOS Lemmings, a faithful PalmOS port of the classic puzzle game Lemmings.</span><span class='zh'>Aaron Ardiri 发布了 PalmOS Lemmings，这是对经典解谜游戏 Lemmings 的 PalmOS 版本移植，展示了对复古游戏的保护与在老旧移动平台上运行早期软件的热情。</span></p>

It highlights retro game preservation and the ongoing hobbyist effort to run vintage software on aging mobile platforms.</span><span class='zh'>Aaron Ardiri 发布了 PalmOS Lemmings，这是对经典解谜游戏 Lemmings 的 PalmOS 版本移植，展示了对复古游戏的保护与在老旧移动平台上运行早期软件的热情。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/shitter/shitter" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shitter – A Nitter Fork</span><span class='zh'>Shitter：Nitter 的一个分叉实现</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shitter is a fork of Nitter, offering a privacy-conscious, JS-light front-end for Twitter-like content and a self-hosted viewing experience.</span><span class='zh'>Shitter 是 Nitter 的一个分叉，提供一个轻量、注重隐私的前端来浏览类似 Twitter 的内容，并支持自托管使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shitter is a fork of Nitter, offering a privacy-conscious, JS-light front-end for Twitter-like content and a self-hosted viewing experience. It exemplifies open-source efforts to reduce tracking and API reliance while preserving access to public posts.</span><span class='zh'>Shitter 是 Nitter 的一个分叉，提供一个轻量、注重隐私的前端来浏览类似 Twitter 的内容，并支持自托管使用。该项目展示了开源社区在减少跟踪、降低对官方 API 依赖方面的努力，同时保持对公开内容的访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PowderworksCode/headstart" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Emitting metadata early makes building/checking Rust up to twice as fast</span><span class='zh'>提前输出元数据使 Rust 构建与检查速度提升至两倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Emitting metadata early reduces repeated work during compilation, speeding up the development loop.</span><span class='zh'>提前输出元数据显著减少编译过程中的重复工作，加速增量构建并提升反馈速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Emitting metadata early reduces repeated work during compilation, speeding up the development loop. This approach can halve build and check times, sharpening feedback for changes and guiding compiler design.</span><span class='zh'>提前输出元数据显著减少编译过程中的重复工作，加速增量构建并提升反馈速度。此举对构建系统和编译器设计也有启示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/otya128/winevdm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>16-bit Windows (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0, 3.1, etc.) on 64-bit Windows</span><span class='zh'>在64位 Windows 上运行16位 Windows 应用（1.x/2.x/3.0/3.1 等）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime. It illustrates how modern Windows still lacks native 16-bit support, leaving preservation-focused projects to recreate the old environment. This matters for running legacy software, archaeology of software, and niche users who still rely on early Windows programs.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。与现代 Windows 不再原生支持16位应用不同，兼容层/运行时的实现让老旧软件得以保存与使用，但仍存在兼容性与性能限制。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PSPi 6 integrates a Raspberry Pi inside a PSP shell, enabling portable retro computing with modern hardware. The project demonstrates clever hardware hacking and makes DIY handheld experimentation more accessible. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andunieee/flipflop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Flipflop – Iroh-powered persistent tunnels for filesharing</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Flipflop——由 Iroh 驱动的用于文件共享的持久隧道</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flipflop demonstrates persistent, reliable tunnels for file sharing powered by Iroh, enabling peers to stay reachable despite NATs and dynamic IPs.</span><span class='zh'>Flipflop 展示了由 Iroh 支持的持久隧道文件共享方案，即使在 NAT 和动态 IP 下也能保持可达。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flipflop demonstrates persistent, reliable tunnels for file sharing powered by Iroh, enabling peers to stay reachable despite NATs and dynamic IPs. The project emphasizes resilient, privacy-friendly file transfer with a simple interface for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Flipflop 展示了由 Iroh 支持的持久隧道文件共享方案，即使在 NAT 和动态 IP 下也能保持可达。该项目强调韧性、隐私友好且对开发者友好的文件传输体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kvoltmer/Audionaut" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Audionaut – an open-source cross-platform multitrack audio editor</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Audionaut——一个开源跨平台的多轨音频编辑器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Audionaut is an open-source, cross-platform multitrack audio editor that aims to empower creators with a community-driven alternative to proprietary tools.</span><span class='zh'>Audionaut 是一个开源、跨平台的多轨音频编辑器，旨在为创作者提供开源替代品，促进社区协作与快速迭代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Audionaut is an open-source, cross-platform multitrack audio editor that aims to empower creators with a community-driven alternative to proprietary tools. It supports recording, editing, and mixing workflows across Windows, macOS, and Linux, inviting collaboration and rapid feature iteration. This project signals a broader move toward open-source, customizable audio tooling for indie producers.</span><span class='zh'>Audionaut 是一个开源、跨平台的多轨音频编辑器，旨在为创作者提供开源替代品，促进社区协作与快速迭代。它支持 Windows、macOS 和 Linux，覆盖录制、编辑、混音等工作流程，鼓励共同开发新功能。该项目代表了开源音频工具在独立创作者生态中的潜在增长。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bherbruck/solidcraft" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: SolidCraft – Open-Source Parametric CAD in Rust (With MCP Support)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：SolidCraft——用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD（含 MCP 支持）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SolidCraft is an open-source, Rust-based parametric CAD project with MCP support, enabling scriptable, reproducible design workflows.</span><span class='zh'>SolidCraft 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD 项目，且支持 MCP，便于实现可脚本化、可重复的设计工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SolidCraft is an open-source, Rust-based parametric CAD project with MCP support, enabling scriptable, reproducible design workflows. By leveraging Rust’s performance and safety, it targets developers who want fast, reliable CAD tooling without relying on heavy C++ stacks. The project signals growing interest in modern, open tooling for mechanical design.</span><span class='zh'>SolidCraft 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源参数化 CAD 项目，且支持 MCP，便于实现可脚本化、可重复的设计工作流。利用 Rust 的性能与安全性，它面向需要快速、可靠 CAD 工具的开发者。该项目反映了对现代、开源机械设计工具日益增长的需求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An AI-assisted formal proof establishes the optimal packing of 11 equal squares, showcasing how AI search and formal verification can tackle challenging geometry problems.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助的严格证明确定了11个相等正方形的最优铺放方案，展示了 AI 搜索与形式化验证结合解决几何难题的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An AI-assisted formal proof establishes the optimal packing of 11 equal squares, showcasing how AI search and formal verification can tackle challenging geometry problems. The work demonstrates the potential of hybrid human-AI reasoning in mathematics.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助的严格证明确定了11个相等正方形的最优铺放方案，展示了 AI 搜索与形式化验证结合解决几何难题的潜力。</span></p>
