<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SaarthurR/atticus-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Atticus – Hold-to-talk voice assistant for macOS that runs on Claude -p</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Atticus——在 macOS 上运行的按住说话的语音助手，基于 Claude-p</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Atticus is an open-source macOS voice assistant that uses Claude with a &#x27;hold-to-talk&#x27; interface, enabling convenient, hands-free interaction.</span><span class='zh'>Atticus 是一个开源的 macOS 语音助手，采用 Claude-p 提供后台服务并使用“按住说话”的交互模式，便捷直观。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Atticus is an open-source macOS voice assistant that uses Claude with a &#x27;hold-to-talk&#x27; interface, enabling convenient, hands-free interaction. It demonstrates modular, privacy-friendly AI tooling on the desktop with a popular LLM backend.</span><span class='zh'>Atticus 是一个开源的 macOS 语音助手，采用 Claude-p 提供后台服务并使用“按住说话”的交互模式，便捷直观。展示了在桌面端使用主流大语言模型后端的模块化、注重隐私的 AI 工具链。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/12/top_stories_10122026'><span class='en'>Oct 12</span><span class='zh'>10月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>108</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049588' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>23</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50160877" data-id="50160877" data-tags="Security|Programming|Code" data-score="102" data-comments="41" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160877" data-time="1792465507">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1176</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go will reject certificate chains containing eight distrusted SPKIs</span><span class='zh'>Go 将拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries. The change reduces attack surface from compromised keys and misissued certificates, nudging developers to audit complex chains or intermediates.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。此举降低了被入侵密钥和被篡改证书带来的风险，促使开发者审查包含多中间证书的证书链。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mottopanikeiku/doomformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I Turned DOOM into a Transformer</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把 DOOM 变成了 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game. It’s a compact, proof-of-concept that sparks ideas about cross-domain AI experiments.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。这个小型的实现是跨领域 AI 实验的一个有趣原型，值得关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Geramy/LemonSeed-Studio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running the unmodified Linux amdgpu driver on an iPad</span><span class='zh'>在 iPad 上运行未修改的 Linux amdgpu 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental project demonstrates loading the unmodified amdgpu driver on iPad hardware, highlighting the gaps between Linux driver support and consumer devices.</span><span class='zh'>一项实验性尝试在 iPad 硬件上运行未修改的 amdgpu 驱动，揭示了 Linux 驱动与消费级设备之间的差距。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental project demonstrates loading the unmodified amdgpu driver on iPad hardware, highlighting the gaps between Linux driver support and consumer devices. It underlines the significant hurdles in achieving functional GPU acceleration and full system usability on non-traditional hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一项实验性尝试在 iPad 硬件上运行未修改的 amdgpu 驱动，揭示了 Linux 驱动与消费级设备之间的差距。强调在非传统硬件上实现显卡加速与完整系统可用性所面临的重大挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/tree/main/preprints/Integer-multiplication-below-n-log-n-September-23-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Integer multiplication below n log n</span><span class='zh'>低于 n log n 的整数乘法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A theoretical preprint claims a method for integer multiplication that beats the classic n log n bound, hinting at new algorithmic ideas in number theory.</span><span class='zh'>理论预印本提出在某些情形下的整数乘法复杂度可低于经典的 n log n 上界，揭示了新的算法思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A theoretical preprint claims a method for integer multiplication that beats the classic n log n bound, hinting at new algorithmic ideas in number theory. If validated, it could influence cryptography and numeric algorithms.</span><span class='zh'>理论预印本提出在某些情形下的整数乘法复杂度可低于经典的 n log n 上界，揭示了新的算法思路。若被验证，其对密码学与数值算法或有影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/owls-on-wires/keplearn" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Keplearn: Extract Equations from Data in Seconds</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn：秒级从数据中提取方程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems. This accelerates scientific experimentation and can complement traditional regression with interpretable models.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。这加速了科学实验，并可与传统回归方法结合，产生可解释的模型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alex0ptr/once" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Once: Cache CLI commands</span><span class='zh'>Once：缓存 CLI 命令</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Once is a CLI utility that caches your frequently used shell commands for quick reuse.</span><span class='zh'>Once 是一个命令行工具，用于缓存常用命令以便快速重用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Once is a CLI utility that caches your frequently used shell commands for quick reuse. It speeds up repetitive tasks and improves reproducibility across sessions and machines. The tool targets developers and sysadmins who want smoother, repeatable workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Once 是一个命令行工具，用于缓存常用命令以便快速重用。它可加速重复任务并提升跨会话、跨机器的可重复性，适合开发者与系统管理员构建更顺畅的工作流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zion-sati/NetWasm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>C# now beats Rust on WASM size</span><span class='zh'>C# 在 WASM 大小方面现在比 Rust 更小</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A WASM size comparison suggests C# compiled to WebAssembly can produce smaller binaries than Rust for certain workloads, challenging the assumption that Rust always yields the smallest output.</span><span class='zh'>对比显示在某些工作负载下，C# 编译为 WebAssembly 的二进制体积可能比 Rust 更小，挑战了“Rust 总是最小”的常识。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A WASM size comparison suggests C# compiled to WebAssembly can produce smaller binaries than Rust for certain workloads, challenging the assumption that Rust always yields the smallest output. This influences language choice for wasm-based apps, though ecosystem and performance considerations remain important.</span><span class='zh'>对比显示在某些工作负载下，C# 编译为 WebAssembly 的二进制体积可能比 Rust 更小，挑战了“Rust 总是最小”的常识。对 wasm 应用的语言选择产生影响，但生态和性能等因素仍然重要。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/21/top_stories_10212026'><span class='en'>Oct 21</span><span class='zh'>10月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>86</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175510' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50005608" data-id="50005608" data-tags="AI|Code" data-score="86" data-comments="24" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50005608" data-time="1791466186">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SamsungLabs/LittleBit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sub-1-Bit LLM Compression via Latent Factorization</span><span class='zh'>通过潜在因子分解实现 Sub-1-Bit 的大语言模型压缩</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LittleBit uses latent factorization to compress large language models to sub-1-bit representations, aiming to retain useful inference quality at far smaller sizes.</span><span class='zh'>LittleBit 通过潜在因子分解把大模型压缩到低于1比特的表示，意在在显著减小模型规模的同时维持可用的推理质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LittleBit uses latent factorization to compress large language models to sub-1-bit representations, aiming to retain useful inference quality at far smaller sizes. The approach could unlock running capable LLMs on edge devices and specialized accelerators, expanding deployment scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>LittleBit 通过潜在因子分解把大模型压缩到低于1比特的表示，意在在显著减小模型规模的同时维持可用的推理质量。该方法有望让边缘设备和定制加速器也能部署强大的 LLM，拓展应用场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/omnara-ai/omnara" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Omnara – Open-source alternative to Claude Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Omnara Open source 替代 Claude 管理代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Omnara is an open source alternative to Claude&#x27;s Managed Agents, offering configurable AI agents that you can run and customize yourself.</span><span class='zh'>Omnara 是一个开源替代方案，定位为可本地控管的 Claude Managed Agents 的对等实现，提供可配置的 AI 代理和可插拔集成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Omnara is an open source alternative to Claude&#x27;s Managed Agents, offering configurable AI agents that you can run and customize yourself. The project emphasizes transparency and privacy and aims to match managed agent capabilities with self hosted deployment.</span><span class='zh'>Omnara 是一个开源替代方案，定位为可本地控管的 Claude Managed Agents 的对等实现，提供可配置的 AI 代理和可插拔集成。该项目强调透明度、可定制性和隐私，面向希望自托管 AI 自动化的团队。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</span><span class='zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anubhavmalhotra/r2d2-tv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Making a smart TV dumb and then smart</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把智能电视变得更“傻”再重新变为智能</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A DIY project that strips embedded smart TV software to create a privacy-preserving, lean device, then reintroduces smart capabilities in a more controllable way.</span><span class='zh'>一个 DIY 项目通过移除智能电视的内置软件来打造一个轻量、注重隐私的设备，随后再以更可控的方式重新引入智能功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A DIY project that strips embedded smart TV software to create a privacy-preserving, lean device, then reintroduces smart capabilities in a more controllable way. The effort highlights the tension between vendor-bloat and user autonomy, and points to hackable hardware routes for better privacy.</span><span class='zh'>一个 DIY 项目通过移除智能电视的内置软件来打造一个轻量、注重隐私的设备，随后再以更可控的方式重新引入智能功能。此举凸显厂商臃肿软件与用户自主权之间的矛盾，并指向可 Hack 的硬件方式以提升隐私保护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/net4people/bbs/issues/672" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Xray-core concealed a certificate verification bypass vulnerability</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 隐藏的证书验证漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks.</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks. The finding underscores the importance of verifying all certificate handling in proxy software and applying patches.</span><span class='zh'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。这一发现强调了代理软件中对证书处理的严格验证和及时打补丁的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dfd-tud/deda" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DEDA – Tracking Dots Extraction, Decoding and Anonymisation Toolkit</span><span class='zh'>DEDA：跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化工具包</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DEDA provides a toolkit for extracting, decoding, and anonymizing tracking dots, enabling researchers to study marker-based tracking and improve privacy protections.</span><span class='zh'>DEDA 提供一个跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化的工具包，帮助研究者研究基于标记的跟踪并提升隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DEDA provides a toolkit for extracting, decoding, and anonymizing tracking dots, enabling researchers to study marker-based tracking and improve privacy protections. It highlights practical methods for analyzing and mitigating telemetry signals.</span><span class='zh'>DEDA 提供一个跟踪点提取、解码与匿名化的工具包，帮助研究者研究基于标记的跟踪并提升隐私保护。它展示了分析与缓解遥测信号的实际方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rsutcliffe/nightwatch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Nightwatch – a Mac menu-bar app that tells you when tonight is clear</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Nightwatch——一个在 Mac 菜单栏显示今晚是否晴朗的应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nightwatch is a Mac menu-bar utility that uses weather and astronomy data to tell you when the night will be clear.</span><span class='zh'>Nightwatch 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，利用天气与天文数据指示今晚是否晴朗，为星空观测等活动提供实用辅助。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nightwatch is a Mac menu-bar utility that uses weather and astronomy data to tell you when the night will be clear. It exemplifies how lightweight tools can add practical value by leveraging public data sources.</span><span class='zh'>Nightwatch 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，利用天气与天文数据指示今晚是否晴朗，为星空观测等活动提供实用辅助。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/morluto/rea" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1209966933/1a3fef9a-be4b-41f4-8830-a94c0e8288cc' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/morluto/rea" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reverse engineer anything with agents, from app behavior down to native binaries</span><span class='zh'>用代理逆向工程：从应用行为到原生二进制</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces a toolkit that uses autonomous agents to reverse engineer software, from observing app behavior to analyzing native binaries.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一个利用自主代理进行逆向工程的工具集，覆盖从分析应用行为到研究原生二进制的全过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces a toolkit that uses autonomous agents to reverse engineer software, from observing app behavior to analyzing native binaries. It demonstrates automation of traditionally manual reverse-engineering tasks and explores risks and capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一个利用自主代理进行逆向工程的工具集，覆盖从分析应用行为到研究原生二进制的全过程。展示了对传统手工逆向工程任务的自动化，并探讨相关风险与潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vibra-Ingenn/Janus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Janus – Go binary that runs GGUF models via Vulkan on AMD/Intel/Nvidia</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Janus – Go binary that runs GGUF models via Vulkan on AMD/Intel/Nvidia</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Janus is a Go-based binary that runs GGUF-formatted models via Vulkan across major GPUs, enabling local AI inference with a single cross-platform tool.</span><span class='zh'>Janus 是一个用 Go 编写的二进制程序，借助 Vulkan 在 AMD/Intel/Nvidia 等显卡上运行 GGUF 模型，实现本地推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Janus is a Go-based binary that runs GGUF-formatted models via Vulkan across major GPUs, enabling local AI inference with a single cross-platform tool. It streamlines setup, supports multiple backends, and lowers the barrier for experiments on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Janus 是一个用 Go 编写的二进制程序，借助 Vulkan 在 AMD/Intel/Nvidia 等显卡上运行 GGUF 模型，实现本地推理。它简化了安装流程，支持多种后端，并降低了在消费级硬件上进行实验的门槛。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN introducing an experimental, pattern-first DAW that supports MCP, signaling a new workflow approach in music production.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 介绍了一个以模式为核心的实验性数字音频工作站，并支持 MCP，预示音乐制作工作流进入一个新阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN introducing an experimental, pattern-first DAW that supports MCP, signaling a new workflow approach in music production. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/theconsensuslabs/noalloc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>noalloc: Tooling and libraries to enforce zero heap allocations in Golang</span><span class='zh'>noalloc：在 Golang 中实现零堆分配的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services. It helps developers reason about memory use, though it may constrain certain idioms and require careful design.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。它帮助开发者管理内存使用，但可能限制某些编码习惯并需谨慎设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/composo-ai/DishwasherBench" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DishwasherBench – Can Frontier Models Stack a Dishwasher?</span><span class='zh'>DishwasherBench：前沿模型能否堆叠一个洗碗机？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DishwasherBench examines whether frontier AI models can interact with and control a real-world appliance like a dishwasher.</span><span class='zh'>DishwasherBench 探索前沿 AI 模型是否能与现实设备（如洗碗机）交互并执行控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DishwasherBench examines whether frontier AI models can interact with and control a real-world appliance like a dishwasher. The project highlights the practical hurdles of getting agents to bridge software planning with hardware actuation.</span><span class='zh'>DishwasherBench 探索前沿 AI 模型是否能与现实设备（如洗碗机）交互并执行控制。该项目揭示了让智能体将软件规划转化为硬件执行所面临的实际难题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/explaindb/explaindb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExplainDB: A Database System Built for Understandability</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB：为可理解性而生的数据库系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExplainDB is a database system designed with understandability in mind, likely offering transparent query planning and explainability features to help developers and operators reason about data flows.</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB 是以可理解性为设计目标的数据库系统，可能提供透明的查询规划和可解释性特性，帮助开发者与运维更清晰地理解数据流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExplainDB is a database system designed with understandability in mind, likely offering transparent query planning and explainability features to help developers and operators reason about data flows.</span><span class='zh'>ExplainDB 是以可理解性为设计目标的数据库系统，可能提供透明的查询规划和可解释性特性，帮助开发者与运维更清晰地理解数据流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/astroautomata/PySR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Symbolic Learning in Python and Julia</span><span class='zh'>Python 与 Julia 中的符号学习</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PySR blends symbolic regression with modern ML ecosystems to yield interpretable models in Python and Julia.</span><span class='zh'>PySR 将符号回归与现代机器学习生态系统相结合，在 Python 与 Julia 中生成可解释的模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PySR blends symbolic regression with modern ML ecosystems to yield interpretable models in Python and Julia. By combining mathematical discovery with scalable tooling, it helps uncover human-readable expressions that fit data.</span><span class='zh'>PySR 将符号回归与现代机器学习生态系统相结合，在 Python 与 Julia 中生成可解释的模型。通过将数学发现与可扩展工具链结合，它有助于发现能拟合数据且易读的表达式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/johnrash/bebox" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku application that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox</span><span class='zh'>BeBox：在 Haiku 上模拟 BeInc 的 BeBox</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku app that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox, allowing retro BeOS-era software to run on modern Haiku OS.</span><span class='zh'>BeBox 是一个在 Haiku 操作系统上运行的 BeBox 模拟应用，允许复古 BeOS 时代的软件在新环境中运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku app that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox, allowing retro BeOS-era software to run on modern Haiku OS. It&#x27;s a niche project that showcases emulation fidelity and the continued appeal of vintage systems. For enthusiasts, it&#x27;s a glimpse into how hobbyists keep old ecosystems alive.</span><span class='zh'>BeBox 是一个在 Haiku 操作系统上运行的 BeBox 模拟应用，允许复古 BeOS 时代的软件在新环境中运行。该项目展示了仿真与系统级再现的潜力，也反映出爱好者对旧系统的持续热情。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/p10node/k10s" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: K10s – A Clickable Kubernetes TUI (Go, Bubble Tea)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：K10s——一个可点击的 Kubernetes TUI（Go、Bubble Tea）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>K10s is a clickable terminal UI for Kubernetes built with Go and Bubble Tea, enabling point-and-click operations inside a TUI instead of pure command lines.</span><span class='zh'>K10s 是一个用 Go 和 Bubble Tea 构建的可点击 Kubernetes 终端界面，允许在 TUI 内进行点选操作而非仅用命令行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>K10s is a clickable terminal UI for Kubernetes built with Go and Bubble Tea, enabling point-and-click operations inside a TUI instead of pure command lines. By combining a familiar CLI domain with a UI that can be navigated via keyboard or mouse, it aims to reduce the cognitive load of managing clusters in the terminal. This project highlights how terminal interfaces can augment traditional DevOps tooling without sacrificing portability.</span><span class='zh'>K10s 是一个用 Go 和 Bubble Tea 构建的可点击 Kubernetes 终端界面，允许在 TUI 内进行点选操作而非仅用命令行。它将熟悉的命令行领域与可键盘/鼠标导航的 UI 结合，旨在降低终端管理集群的认知负担。该项目展示了终端工具如何在不牺牲可移植性的前提下变得更易用。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Parin is a 2D game engine written in D, positioned between the simplicity of raylib and the featureset of Godot, aiming for a balance of ease of use and flexibility.</span><span class='zh'>Parin 是一款用 D 语言编写的二维游戏引擎，介于 raylib 的简洁和 Godot 的功能性之间，力求在易用性与灵活性之间取得平衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mikesart/gpuvis" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>gpuvis: GPU Trace Visualizer</span><span class='zh'>gpuvis：GPU 跟踪可视化工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>gpuvis turns GPU trace logs into interactive timelines, helping developers pinpoint where rendering or compute bottlenecks occur.</span><span class='zh'>gpuvis 将 GPU 跟踪日志转化为交互式时间线，帮助开发者定位渲染或计算瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>gpuvis turns GPU trace logs into interactive timelines, helping developers pinpoint where rendering or compute bottlenecks occur. By visualizing GPU activity, it simplifies performance tuning across graphics pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>gpuvis 将 GPU 跟踪日志转化为交互式时间线，帮助开发者定位渲染或计算瓶颈。通过直观的可视化，简化了图形管线的性能调优。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bealesh/neckbeard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neckbeard – A DevOps/platform/SRE plugin for your agent</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard——为你的代理提供的 DevOps/平台/SRE 插件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation. It aims to make agents more useful and safer in operational contexts by providing ready-made connectors and operational primitives.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。它通过提供现成的连接器和操作原语，使代理在运行时管理场景中更实用且更安全。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/moritzwilksch/diffle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Diffle, a keyboard-centric diff viewer with LSP support</span><span class='zh'>Diffle：以键盘为中心的 Diff 查看器，支持 LSP</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Diffle is a diff viewer designed for keyboard-first navigation with added LSP support for code-aware diffs.</span><span class='zh'>Diffle 是一个以键盘优先为设计的差异查看工具，并集成了对 LSP 的支持，提升对 diffs 的代码感知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Diffle is a diff viewer designed for keyboard-first navigation with added LSP support for code-aware diffs. It aims to speed up reviews and patch creation by minimizing mouse use and providing inline intelligence. Takeaway: keyboard-centric tools with code intelligence can accelerate code comparison workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Diffle 是一个以键盘优先为设计的差异查看工具，并集成了对 LSP 的支持，提升对 diffs 的代码感知。通过最小化鼠标使用并提供代码智能，能加速审查和补丁编写。要点是键盘优先的工具结合代码智能能提升效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/arterialist/kube32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I rebuilt Kubernetes for ESP32 microcontrollers</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我为 ESP32 微控制器重做了 Kubernetes</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers. The project lets kubectl and k9s manage boards over Wi-Fi or USB serial, while pods run WebAssembly modules through the wasm3 interpreter. A board with PSRAM serves as the control plane, and boards without PSRAM act as workers. The same sources also build a macOS and Linux host simulator.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。该项目支持通过 Wi-Fi 或 USB 串口使用 kubectl 和 k9s 管理开发板，并通过 wasm3 解释器运行 WebAssembly 模块。带 PSRAM 的开发板充当控制平面，不带 PSRAM 的开发板作为工作节点。同一套源代码还可构建 macOS 和 Linux 主机模拟器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ymcrcat/rgpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Rgpu – a PyTorch device whose tensors live on a remote GPU</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Rgpu – 一个让张量驻留在远程GPU上的 PyTorch 设备</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rgpu is a PyTorch device where tensors live on a remote GPU, enabling offloading and potential speedups for large models.</span><span class='zh'>Rgpu 是一个让张量驻留在远程 GPU 的 PyTorch 设备，支持将计算分发到另一台主机以进行加速。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rgpu is a PyTorch device where tensors live on a remote GPU, enabling offloading and potential speedups for large models. It highlights tradeoffs in latency, bandwidth, and distributed computation.</span><span class='zh'>Rgpu 是一个让张量驻留在远程 GPU 的 PyTorch 设备，支持将计算分发到另一台主机以进行加速。它揭示了延迟、带宽与分布式计算的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vacuula/vacuula" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Custom server to revive neato bots</span><span class='zh'>自定义服务器让 Neato 机器人重新工作</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open-source project provides a custom server to revive Neato robots by replacing or augmenting the official backend, enabling continued control and firmware updates.</span><span class='zh'>一个开源项目提供自定义服务器，通过替代或增强官方后端，让 Neato 机器人继续被控制并获得固件更新，展现了社区驱动的硬件改造如何延长消费物联网设备的寿命。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open-source project provides a custom server to revive Neato robots by replacing or augmenting the official backend, enabling continued control and firmware updates. This demonstrates how community-driven hardware hacking can extend the life of consumer IoT devices.</span><span class='zh'>一个开源项目提供自定义服务器，通过替代或增强官方后端，让 Neato 机器人继续被控制并获得固件更新，展现了社区驱动的硬件改造如何延长消费物联网设备的寿命。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/clickhouse-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ClickHouse C – Talk to ClickHouse using its native binary protocol</span><span class='zh'>ClickHouse C：用原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 通信</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Announces a C library that talks to ClickHouse using the database&#x27;s native binary protocol, reducing overhead and simplifying integration from C applications.</span><span class='zh'>推出一个用于 C 的 ClickHouse 客户端库，通过原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 交互，降低开销并简化在 C 应用中的集成，提升数据写入与查询效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Announces a C library that talks to ClickHouse using the database&#x27;s native binary protocol, reducing overhead and simplifying integration from C applications. This streamlines high-performance data ingestion and querying for C ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>推出一个用于 C 的 ClickHouse 客户端库，通过原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 交互，降低开销并简化在 C 应用中的集成，提升数据写入与查询效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Partyline is a group voice app built on Reticulum and LXST, showcasing a decentralized approach to real-time group audio.</span><span class='zh'>Partyline 是一个在 Reticulum 与 LXST 上构建的群组语音应用，展示了去中心化的实时群体音频通信。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Partyline is a group voice app built on Reticulum and LXST, showcasing a decentralized approach to real-time group audio. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/graphicle-ai/graphicle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Graphicle, a Rust port of Graphify with memory for coding agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Graphicle——Graphify 的 Rust 移植，带有编码代理记忆</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Graphicle is a Rust port of Graphify that adds a memory model tailored for coding agents, enabling them to retain context and past decisions during multi-step programming tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Graphicle 是 Graphify 的 Rust 移植版，增加了为编码代理设计的记忆模型，使代理在多步骤编程任务中能保留上下文与历史决策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Graphicle is a Rust port of Graphify that adds a memory model tailored for coding agents, enabling them to retain context and past decisions during multi-step programming tasks. Implementing this in Rust emphasizes performance and safety, making it attractive for systems that need reliable, low-latency agent-assisted coding workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Graphicle 是 Graphify 的 Rust 移植版，增加了为编码代理设计的记忆模型，使代理在多步骤编程任务中能保留上下文与历史决策。用 Rust 实现强调性能与安全，适合需要可靠、低延迟代理辅助编码的系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/superdesigndev/treg" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Treg (OpenRouter for Tools)</span><span class='zh'>Treg：工具的开放路由器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Treg is an open router for tools, enabling dynamic discovery and routing of tool invocations in AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Treg 是一个面向工具的开源路由器，支持在 AI 工作流中动态发现并路由工具调用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Treg is an open router for tools, enabling dynamic discovery and routing of tool invocations in AI workflows. It helps organize, route, and orchestrate tool calls, reducing integration boilerplate and enabling scalable pipelines for agents.</span><span class='zh'>Treg 是一个面向工具的开源路由器，支持在 AI 工作流中动态发现并路由工具调用。它整理、分发和编排工具调用，降低集成工作量，提升代理系统的可扩展性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xscreensaver-official/xscreensaver" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>There is no official XScreenSaver GitHub repository</span><span class='zh'>没有官方的 XScreenSaver GitHub 仓库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>XScreenSaver lacks an official GitHub repository, leaving users to rely on forks and mirrors.</span><span class='zh'>XScreenSaver 缺乏官方 GitHub 仓库，用户只能依赖 forks 与镜像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>XScreenSaver lacks an official GitHub repository, leaving users to rely on forks and mirrors. The situation highlights how long-running open-source projects drift from centralized hosting, complicating updates and issue tracking.</span><span class='zh'>XScreenSaver 缺乏官方 GitHub 仓库，用户只能依赖 forks 与镜像。本质上反映了长期运行的开源项目可能渐渐远离集中托管，更新与问题跟踪变得更加困难。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/4yk5/Ghostman" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Ghostman – A fast, keyboard-driven TUI API client and webhook inspector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Ghostman——快速、键盘驱动的 TUI API 客户端与 Webhook 检查器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ghostman is a keyboard-driven API client and webhook inspector that runs in the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostman 是一个运行在终端中的键盘驱动 API 客户端和 Webhook 检查器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghostman is a keyboard-driven API client and webhook inspector that runs in the terminal. It is local-first, requires no cloud account, uses no Electron, and is presented as a lightweight alternative to heavier modern API clients.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostman 是一个运行在终端中的键盘驱动 API 客户端和 Webhook 检查器。它以本地优先为设计理念，无需云账户，也不使用 Electron，定位是更轻量的现代 API 客户端替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/awslabs/aidlc-workflows" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Aidlc: Adaptive workflow steering rules for AI coding agents</span><span class='zh'>Aidlc：用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aidlc introduces adaptive workflow steering rules to guide AI coding agents, aiming to improve safety, reliability, and efficiency in code generation.</span><span class='zh'>Aidlc 提出用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则，旨在提升代码生成的安全性、稳健性与效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Aidlc introduces adaptive workflow steering rules to guide AI coding agents, aiming to improve safety, reliability, and efficiency in code generation. The framework uses dynamic policies to adapt to tasks and context, reducing riskier operations.</span><span class='zh'>Aidlc 提出用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则，旨在提升代码生成的安全性、稳健性与效率。通过动态策略调整任务与上下文，降低风险操作的发生。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hibanaworks/hibana" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Choreographic Programming in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Rust 的编舞式编程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Choreographic programming applies global specifications to coordinate distributed components.</span><span class='zh'>舞蹈式编程通过全局规范来协调分布式组件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Choreographic programming applies global specifications to coordinate distributed components. The Rust project demonstrates how to express choreography as code, enabling safer composition of interacting modules. It offers a path to modeling concurrent systems with formal constraints while leveraging Rust&#x27;s type system.</span><span class='zh'>舞蹈式编程通过全局规范来协调分布式组件。该 Rust 项目展示了将编排作为代码书写，从而实现对交互模块的更安全组合。此方向为用形式化约束建模并发系统提供了一条利用 Rust 类型系统的路线。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/12/top_stories_10122026'><span class='en'>Oct 12</span><span class='zh'>10月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>24</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50042936' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>15</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-49916997" data-id="49916997" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="24" data-comments="15" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49916997" data-time="1790821848">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TantrixAuto/yantra" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Yantra – an LALR(1) parser generator for C++</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yantra — 面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yantra is a new LALR(1) parser generator for C++.</span><span class='zh'>Yantra 是一个面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器，旨在通过输出整洁、符合现代 C++ 风格的代码与解析表来简化解析器开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yantra is a new LALR(1) parser generator for C++. It aims to streamline building language parsers by emitting clean, idiomatic C++ code and parsing tables. For projects where tight integration with modern C++ tooling matters, it offers an alternative to traditional parser generators with a focus on ergonomics and performance.</span><span class='zh'>Yantra 是一个面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器，旨在通过输出整洁、符合现代 C++ 风格的代码与解析表来简化解析器开发。在需要与现代 C++ 工具链紧密集成的场景中，它提供了一个对传统生成器的高效、易用的替代方案。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/01/top_stories_10012026'><span class='en'>Oct 1</span><span class='zh'>10月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>24</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49916997' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>15</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50195535" data-id="50195535" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="23" data-comments="13" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195535" data-time="1792678068">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmadeusITGroup/Edison" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Parsing EDIFACT in Python with syntax trees</span><span class='zh'>在 Python 中用语法树解析 EDIFACT</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Edison provides a Python toolkit to parse EDIFACT messages using syntax trees, enabling more readable, verifiable parsing of standardized EDI data.</span><span class='zh'>Edison 提供了一个用语法树解析 EDIFACT 的 Python 工具包，使对标准化EDI数据的解析更加直观且可验证。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Edison provides a Python toolkit to parse EDIFACT messages using syntax trees, enabling more readable, verifiable parsing of standardized EDI data. This approach helps developers validate and transform large batch files used in travel, logistics, and commerce.</span><span class='zh'>Edison 提供了一个用语法树解析 EDIFACT 的 Python 工具包，使对标准化EDI数据的解析更加直观且可验证。该方法有助于在旅行业、物流和商业领域处理的大规模文件进行验证与转换。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/24/top_stories_10242026'><span class='en'>Oct 24</span><span class='zh'>10月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>23</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195535' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50235449" data-id="50235449" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="21" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235449" data-time="1792958013">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-research/kauldron" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kauldron: Modular, scalable library to train ML models</span><span class='zh'>Kauldron：可模块化、可扩展的 ML 模型训练库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/ethical-foss/open-slopware" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>open-slopware – Alternatives to FOSS projects choosing to use LLMs/AI</span><span class='zh'>open-slopware：在选择使用 LLM/AI 的 FOSS 项目中的替代方案</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>open-slopware explores options for FOSS projects that adopt LLMs/AI, weighing openness, licensing, and governance implications.</span><span class='zh'>open-slopware 探讨在采用大型语言模型/人工智能的 FOSS 项目中可以采用的替代路径，权衡开放性、许可与治理的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>open-slopware explores options for FOSS projects that adopt LLMs/AI, weighing openness, licensing, and governance implications. It highlights how AI integration can complicate traditional OSS philosophies.</span><span class='zh'>open-slopware 探讨在采用大型语言模型/人工智能的 FOSS 项目中可以采用的替代路径，权衡开放性、许可与治理的影响。强调 AI 集成如何给传统开源理念带来新的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Netflix/vmaf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Netflix vmaf: Perceptual video quality assessment based on multi-method fusion</span><span class='zh'>Netflix VMAF：基于多方法融合的主观感知视频质量评估</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Netflix VMAF blends several perceptual quality metrics into a single score, improving correlation with viewer experience across encoding settings.</span><span class='zh'>Netflix VMAF 将多种感知质量指标融合为一个综合分数，在不同编码设置下与用户观感的相关性更高，提升了流媒体的质量评估与编解码评估能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Netflix VMAF blends several perceptual quality metrics into a single score, improving correlation with viewer experience across encoding settings. The project shows how multi-method fusion enhances streaming QA and codec evaluation.</span><span class='zh'>Netflix VMAF 将多种感知质量指标融合为一个综合分数，在不同编码设置下与用户观感的相关性更高，提升了流媒体的质量评估与编解码评估能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Koppar-AI/koppar-lte-modem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A USB-C LTE modem that gives my computer a phone line</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一款通过 USB-C 为电脑提供电话线路的 LTE 调制解调器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open hardware board gives a computer LTE data, SMS, and voice calls over USB-C.</span><span class='zh'>这款开源硬件板通过 USB-C 为电脑提供 LTE 数据、短信和语音通话功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open hardware board gives a computer LTE data, SMS, and voice calls over USB-C. With a nano-SIM and one USB-C cable, a computer can route call audio to a headset, hand calls to a script or AI agent, and send texts. The four-layer KiCad board was designed from a one-page specification and reviewed against component datasheets.</span><span class='zh'>这款开源硬件板通过 USB-C 为电脑提供 LTE 数据、短信和语音通话功能。插入 nano-SIM 并连接一根 USB-C 线后，电脑可以把通话音频发送到耳机，也可以将通话交给脚本或 AI 代理处理，并发送短信。这块四层 KiCad 电路板根据一页规格说明设计，并逐项对照元件数据手册进行审核。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/skorotkiewicz/radio-in-a-box" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Radio-in-a-box: Self-hosted internet radio with Icecast, Liquidsoap and Rust CLI</span><span class='zh'>Radio-in-a-box：使用 Icecast、Liquidsoap 和 Rust CLI 的自托管网络广播箱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A self-hosted radio stack that combines Icecast, Liquidsoap, and a Rust-based CLI to simplify running your own Internet radio.</span><span class='zh'>自托管的网络广播堆栈，将 Icecast、Liquidsoap 与 Rust CLI 组合在一起，简化自建互联网电台的部署。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A self-hosted radio stack that combines Icecast, Liquidsoap, and a Rust-based CLI to simplify running your own Internet radio. The project emphasizes portability, reproducibility, and a minimal maintenance footprint for hobbyists.</span><span class='zh'>自托管的网络广播堆栈，将 Icecast、Liquidsoap 与 Rust CLI 组合在一起，简化自建互联网电台的部署。强调可移植性、可重复性与低维护成本，面向爱好者。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2100</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lzprograma/Chip-Hexa" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chip-hex:cost-benefit, energy efficiency and hexagonal</span><span class='zh'>Chip-Hexa：成本、能效与六边形布局</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chip-Hexa surveys the potential benefits of hexagonal chip layouts, arguing that hex tiling can improve interconnect density, energy efficiency, and cooling compared with traditional square grids.</span><span class='zh'>Chip-Hexa 探讨六边形芯片布局在成本、能耗与互连密度方面的潜在优势，比较六边形与传统方格的取舍，提出未来芯片架构的设计思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chip-Hexa surveys the potential benefits of hexagonal chip layouts, arguing that hex tiling can improve interconnect density, energy efficiency, and cooling compared with traditional square grids. It weighs design and fabrication trade-offs, offering ideas for future chip architectures.</span><span class='zh'>Chip-Hexa 探讨六边形芯片布局在成本、能耗与互连密度方面的潜在优势，比较六边形与传统方格的取舍，提出未来芯片架构的设计思路。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/10/top_stories_10102026'><span class='en'>Oct 10</span><span class='zh'>10月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>3</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019988' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50315782" data-id="50315782" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Programming|Code" data-score="3" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782" data-time="1793451509">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2101</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A language model that lives in a boot sector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：运行在引导扇区中的语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project fits an entire inference engine into a 510-byte MBR, with the model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in following sectors.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目将完整推理引擎装进510字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词表和字符串存放在后续扇区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project fits an entire inference engine into a 510-byte MBR, with the model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in following sectors. A small character-level neural network trained on machine-generated dmesg output can therefore produce Linux boot messages indefinitely. The accompanying Python trainer performs the same integer arithmetic as the assembly implementation and matches its output character by character. Debugging exposed an x86 idiv instruction overwriting the edx register used for the bias-table pointer, causing the first neuron to be wrong.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目将完整推理引擎装进510字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词表和字符串存放在后续扇区。一个使用机器生成的 dmesg 输出训练的小型字符级神经网络，因此可以无限生成 Linux 启动消息。配套的 Python 训练程序执行与汇编实现相同的整数运算，输出能够逐字符匹配。调试发现，x86 的 idiv 指令覆盖了原本用于偏置表指针的 edx 寄存器，导致第一个神经元出错。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>3</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article></main><footer class='foot wrap'>Hacker News Daily · <span class='en'>Source</span><span class='zh'>来源</span>: <a href='https://news.ycombinator.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>news.ycombinator.com</a> · <a href='/hackernews/feed.xml'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M4 11a9 9 0 0 1 9 9M4 4a16 16 0 0 1 16 16'/><circle cx='5' cy='19' r='1'/></svg> RSS</a></footer><nav class='tabbar'><a class='' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M3 10.5 12 3l9 7.5V21h-6v-6H9v6H3z'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Today</span><span class='zh'>今日</span></span></a><a class='on' href='/hackernews/#archive'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><rect x='3' y='4' width='18' height='18' rx='2'/><path d='M16 2v4M8 2v4M3 10h18'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Archive</span><span class='zh'>往期</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/trends/'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M3 3v18h18'/><path d='m7 15 4-4 3 3 6-7'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Trends</span><span class='zh'>趋势</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/favorites/'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='m12 2 3.1 6.3 6.9 1-5 4.9 1.2 6.8L12 17.8 5.8 21l1.2-6.8-5-4.9 6.9-1z'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Saved</span><span class='zh'>收藏</span></span></a></nav>
